639 |     if (createSourceMap) {

641 |     }

===== M0006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldAssign(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 450-520
Documentation:

Method code:
450 |   private Node tryFoldAssign(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
451 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.getType() == Token.ASSIGN);
452 | 
453 |     // Tries to convert x = x + y -> x += y;
454 |     if (!right.hasChildren() ||
455 |         right.getFirstChild().getNext() != right.getLastChild()) {
456 |       // RHS must have two children.
457 |       return n;
458 |     }
459 | 
460 |     if (mayHaveSideEffects(left)) {
461 |       return n;
462 |     }
463 | 
464 |     Node newRight;
465 |     if (areNodesEqualForInlining(left, right.getFirstChild())) {
466 |       newRight = right.getLastChild();
467 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isCommutative(right.getType()) &&
468 |           areNodesEqualForInlining(left, right.getLastChild())) {
469 |       newRight = right.getFirstChild();
470 |     } else {
471 |       return n;
472 |     }
473 | 
474 |     int newType = -1;
475 |     switch (right.getType()) {
476 |       case Token.ADD:
477 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_ADD;
478 |         break;
479 |       case Token.BITAND:
480 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITAND;
481 |         break;
482 |       case Token.BITOR:
483 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITOR;
484 |         break;
485 |       case Token.BITXOR:
486 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR;
487 |         break;
488 |       case Token.DIV:
489 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_DIV;
490 |         break;
491 |       case Token.LSH:
492 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_LSH;
493 |         break;
494 |       case Token.MOD:
495 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MOD;
496 |         break;
497 |       case Token.MUL:
498 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_MUL;
499 |         break;
500 |       case Token.RSH:
501 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_RSH;
502 |         break;
503 |       case Token.SUB:
504 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_SUB;
505 |         break;
506 |       case Token.URSH:
507 |         newType = Token.ASSIGN_URSH;
508 |         break;
509 |       default:
510 |         return n;
511 |     }
512 | 
513 |     Node newNode = new Node(newType,
514 |         left.detachFromParent(), newRight.detachFromParent());
515 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, newNode);
516 | 
517 |     reportCodeChange();
518 | 
519 |     return newNode;
520 |   }

50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),

===== M0024 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

===== M0031 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 632-658
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
632 |   @Override
633 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
634 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
635 |       return false;
636 |     }
637 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
638 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
639 |       return false;
640 |     }
641 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
642 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
643 |         return this == that;
644 |       }
645 |       return false;
646 |     }
647 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
648 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
649 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
650 |       }
651 |       return false;
652 |     }
653 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
654 |       return false;
655 |     }
656 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
657 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
658 |   }

===== M0036 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 203-231
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
204 |     maybeEndStatement();
205 | 
206 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
207 |     char prev = getLastChar();
208 | 
209 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
210 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
211 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
212 |       append(" ");
213 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
214 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
215 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
216 |       append(" ");
217 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
218 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     }
221 | 
222 |     // Allow formating around the operator.
223 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
224 | 
225 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
226 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
227 |     // because it looks strange.
228 |     if (binOp) {
229 |       maybeCutLine();
230 |     }
231 |   }

===== M0037 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isNumericResultHelper(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1190-1224
Documentation:

Method code:
1190 |   static boolean isNumericResultHelper(Node n) {
1191 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1192 |       case Token.ADD:
1193 |         return !mayBeString(n.getFirstChild())
1194 |             && !mayBeString(n.getLastChild());
1195 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1196 |       case Token.BITOR:
1197 |       case Token.BITXOR:
1198 |       case Token.BITAND:
1199 |       case Token.LSH:
1200 |       case Token.RSH:
1201 |       case Token.URSH:
1202 |       case Token.SUB:
1203 |       case Token.MUL:
1204 |       case Token.MOD:
1205 |       case Token.DIV:
1206 |       case Token.INC:
1207 |       case Token.DEC:
1208 |       case Token.POS:
1209 |       case Token.NEG:
1210 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1211 |         return true;
1212 |       case Token.NAME:
1213 |         String name = n.getString();
1214 |         if (name.equals("NaN")) {
1215 |           return true;
1216 |         }
1217 |         if (name.equals("Infinity")) {
1218 |           return true;
1219 |         }
1220 |         return false;
1221 |       default:
1222 |         return false;
1223 |     }
1224 |   }

===== M0051 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.BasicErrorManager.LeveledJSErrorComparator.compare(ErrorWithLevel,ErrorWithLevel)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/BasicErrorManager.java
Original range: 120-170
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |     public int compare(ErrorWithLevel p1, ErrorWithLevel p2) {
121 |       // null is the smallest value
122 |       if (p2 == null) {
123 |         if (p1 == null) {
124 |           return 0;
125 |         } else {
126 |           return P1_GT_P2;
127 |         }
128 |       }
129 | 
130 |       // check level
131 |       if (p1.level != p2.level) {
132 |         return p2.level.compareTo(p1.level);
133 |       }
134 | 
135 |       // sourceName comparison
136 |       String source1 = p1.error.sourceName;
137 |       String source2 = p2.error.sourceName;
138 |       if (source1 != null && source2 != null) {
139 |         int sourceCompare = source1.compareTo(source2);
140 |         if (sourceCompare != 0) {
141 |           return sourceCompare;
142 |         }
143 |       } else if (source1 == null && source2 != null) {
144 |         return P1_LT_P2;
145 |       } else if (source1 != null && source2 == null) {
146 |         return P1_GT_P2;
147 |       }
148 |       // lineno comparison
149 |       int lineno1 = p1.error.lineNumber;
150 |       int lineno2 = p2.error.lineNumber;
151 |       if (lineno1 != lineno2) {
152 |         return lineno1 - lineno2;
153 |       } else if (lineno1 < 0 && 0 <= lineno2) {
154 |         return P1_LT_P2;
155 |       } else if (0 <= lineno1 && lineno2 < 0) {
156 |         return P1_GT_P2;
157 |       }
158 |       // charno comparison
159 |       int charno1 = p1.error.getCharno();
160 |       int charno2 = p2.error.getCharno();
161 |       if (charno1 != charno2) {
162 |         return charno1 - charno2;
163 |       } else if (charno1 < 0 && 0 <= charno2) {
164 |         return P1_LT_P2;
165 |       } else if (0 <= charno1 && charno2 < 0) {
166 |         return P1_GT_P2;
167 |       }
168 |       // description
169 |       return p1.error.description.compareTo(p2.error.description);
170 |     }

===== M0064 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryConvertToNumber(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 224-266
Documentation:

Method code:
224 |   private void tryConvertToNumber(Node n) {
225 |     switch (n.getType()) {
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         // Nothing to do
228 |         return;
229 |       case Token.AND:
230 |       case Token.OR:
231 |       case Token.COMMA:
232 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
233 |         return;
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getChildAtIndex(1));
236 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
237 |         return;
238 |       case Token.NAME:
239 |         if (!NodeUtil.isUndefined(n)) {
240 |           return;
241 |         }
242 |         break;
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     Double result = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(n);
246 |     if (result == null) {
247 |       return;
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     double value = result;
251 | 
252 |     Node replacement;
253 |     if (Double.isNaN(value)) {
254 |       replacement = Node.newString(Token.NAME, "NaN");
255 |     } else if (value == Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY) {
256 |       replacement = Node.newString(Token.NAME, "Infinity");
257 |     } else if (value == Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY) {
258 |       replacement = new Node(Token.NEG, Node.newString(Token.NAME, "Infinity"));
259 |       replacement.copyInformationFromForTree(n);
260 |     } else {
261 |       replacement = Node.newNumber(value);
262 |     }
263 | 
264 |     n.getParent().replaceChild(n, replacement);
265 |     reportCodeChange();
266 |   }

775 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {

===== M0071 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1583-1642
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1583 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1584 |     if (type != node.getType()
1585 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1586 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1587 |       return false;
1588 |     }
1589 | 
1590 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1591 |       return false;
1592 |     }
1593 | 
1594 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1595 |       try {
1596 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1597 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1598 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1599 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1600 |             return false;
1601 |           }
1602 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1603 |           return false;
1604 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1605 |           return false;
1606 |         } else {
1607 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1608 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1609 |               return false;
1610 |             }
1611 |           }
1612 |         }
1613 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1614 |         return false;
1615 |       }
1616 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1617 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1618 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1619 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1620 |         return false;
1621 |       }
1622 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1623 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1624 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1625 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1626 |         return false;
1627 |       }
1628 |     }
1629 | 
1630 |     if (recurse) {
1631 |       Node n, n2;
1632 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1633 |            n != null;
1634 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1635 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1636 |           return false;
1637 |         }
1638 |       }
1639 |     }
1640 | 
1641 |     return true;
1642 |   }

===== M0082 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

===== M0087 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.performArithmeticOp(int,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 664-746
Documentation:
/**
   * Try to fold arithmetic binary operators
   */
Method code:
664 |   private Node performArithmeticOp(int opType, Node left, Node right) {
665 |     // Unlike other operations, ADD operands are not always converted
666 |     // to Number.
667 |     if (opType == Token.ADD
668 |         && (NodeUtil.mayBeString(left, false)
669 |             || NodeUtil.mayBeString(right, false))) {
670 |       return null;
671 |     }
672 | 
673 |     double result;
674 | 
675 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Handle NaN with unknown value. BIT ops convert NaN
676 |     // to zero so this is a little akward here.
677 | 
678 |     Double lValObj = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(left);
679 |     if (lValObj == null) {
680 |       return null;
681 |     }
682 |     Double rValObj = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(right);
683 |     if (rValObj == null) {
684 |       return null;
685 |     }
686 | 
687 |     double lval = lValObj;
688 |     double rval = rValObj;
689 | 
690 |     switch (opType) {
691 |       case Token.BITAND:
692 |         result = ScriptRuntime.toInt32(lval) & ScriptRuntime.toInt32(rval);
693 |         break;
694 |       case Token.BITOR:
695 |         result = ScriptRuntime.toInt32(lval) | ScriptRuntime.toInt32(rval);
696 |         break;
697 |       case Token.BITXOR:
698 |         result = ScriptRuntime.toInt32(lval) ^ ScriptRuntime.toInt32(rval);
699 |         break;
700 |       case Token.ADD:
701 |         result = lval + rval;
702 |         break;
703 |       case Token.SUB:
704 |         result = lval - rval;
705 |         break;
706 |       case Token.MUL:
707 |         result = lval * rval;
708 |         break;
709 |       case Token.MOD:
710 |         if (rval == 0) {
711 |           error(DiagnosticType.error("JSC_DIVIDE_BY_0_ERROR", "Divide by 0"), right);
712 |           return null;
713 |         }
714 |         result = lval % rval;
715 |         break;
716 |       case Token.DIV:
717 |         if (rval == 0) {
718 |           error(DiagnosticType.error("JSC_DIVIDE_BY_0_ERROR", "Divide by 0"), right);
719 |           return null;
720 |         }
721 |         result = lval / rval;
722 |         break;
723 |       default:
724 |         throw new Error("Unexpected arithmetic operator");
725 |     }
726 | 
727 |     // TODO(johnlenz): consider removing the result length check.
728 |     // length of the left and right value plus 1 byte for the operator.
729 |     if (String.valueOf(result).length() <=
730 |         String.valueOf(lval).length() + String.valueOf(rval).length() + 1 &&
731 | 
732 |         // Do not try to fold arithmetic for numbers > 2^53. After that
733 |         // point, fixed-point math starts to break down and become inaccurate.
734 |         Math.abs(result) <= MAX_FOLD_NUMBER) {
735 |       Node newNumber = Node.newNumber(result);
736 |       return newNumber;
737 |     } else if (Double.isNaN(result)) {
738 |       return Node.newString(Token.NAME, "NaN");
739 |     } else if (result == Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY) {
740 |       return Node.newString(Token.NAME, "Infinity");
741 |     } else if (result == Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY) {
742 |       return new Node(Token.NEG, Node.newString(Token.NAME, "Infinity"));
743 |     }
744 | 
745 |     return null;
746 |   }

===== M0089 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.ScriptRuntime.getMessage(String,Object[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java
Original range: 456-483
Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {
458 |         final String defaultResource
459 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
460 | 
461 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
462 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 | 
467 |         String formatString;
468 |         try {
469 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
471 |             throw new RuntimeException
472 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
473 |         }
474 | 
475 |         /*
476 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
477 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */
480 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
483 |     }

496 |     registerNativeType(JSTypeNative.U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE, U2U_FUNCTION_TYPE);

499 |     // as instance type
500 |     FunctionType U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE =
501 |         // This is equivalent to

503 |         // in addition, overrides getInstanceType() to return the NoObject type
504 |         // instead of a new anonymous object.

55 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
56 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
57 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
58 |       addGuards(Lists.reverse(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards));
59 |     } else {

61 |       if (index < 0) {

63 |       }
64 |       this.guards.add(index, guard);
65 |     }

===== M0102 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SuppressDocWarningsGuard.level(JSError)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SuppressDocWarningsGuard.java
Original range: 49-86
Documentation:

Method code:
49 |   @Override
50 |   public CheckLevel level(JSError error) {
51 |     Node node = error.node;
52 |     if (node != null) {
53 |       for (Node current = node;
54 |            current != null;
55 |            current = current.getParent()) {
56 |         int type = current.getType();
57 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
58 | 
59 |         // We only care about function annotations at the FUNCTION and SCRIPT
60 |         // level. Otherwise, the @suppress annotation has an implicit
61 |         // dependency on the exact structure of our AST, and that seems like
62 |         // a bad idea.
63 |         if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
64 |           info = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(current);
65 |         } else if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
66 |           info = current.getJSDocInfo();
67 |         }
68 | 
69 |         if (info != null) {
70 |           for (String suppressor : info.getSuppressions()) {
71 |             WarningsGuard guard = suppressors.get(suppressor);
72 | 
73 |             // Some @suppress tags are for other tools, and
74 |             // may not have a warnings guard.
75 |             if (guard != null) {
76 |               CheckLevel newLevel = guard.level(error);
77 |               if (newLevel != null) {
78 |                 return newLevel;
79 |               }
80 |             }
81 |           }
82 |         }
83 |       }
84 |     }
85 |     return null;
86 |   }

===== M0104 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1067-1204
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1067 |   Node parseInputs() {
1068 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1069 | 
1070 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1071 |     // individual file parse trees.
1072 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1073 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1074 |     }
1075 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1076 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1077 |     }
1078 | 
1079 |     // Parse main js sources.
1080 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1081 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1082 | 
1083 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1084 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1085 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1086 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1087 |     }
1088 | 
1089 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1090 | 
1091 |     try {
1092 |       // Parse externs sources.
1093 |       externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1094 |       externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1095 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1096 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1097 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1098 |           return null;
1099 |         }
1100 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1101 |       }
1102 | 
1103 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1104 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1105 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1106 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1107 | 
1108 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1109 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1110 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1111 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1112 |           }
1113 |         }
1114 | 
1115 |         try {
1116 |           inputs =
1117 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1118 |               .manageDependencies(
1119 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1120 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1121 |           report(JSError.make(
1122 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1123 |           return null;
1124 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1125 |           report(JSError.make(
1126 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1127 |           return null;
1128 |         }
1129 |       }
1130 | 
1131 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1132 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1133 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1134 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1135 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1136 |           return null;
1137 |         }
1138 | 
1139 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1140 |         if (n == null) {
1141 |           continue;
1142 |         }
1143 | 
1144 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1145 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1146 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1147 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1148 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1149 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1150 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1151 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1152 | 
1153 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1154 | 
1155 |             externs.add(input);
1156 |             staleInputs = true;
1157 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1158 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1159 |             staleInputs = true;
1160 |           }
1161 |         }
1162 |       }
1163 | 
1164 |       if (staleInputs) {
1165 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1166 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1167 |       }
1168 | 
1169 |       // Build the AST.
1170 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1171 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1172 |         if (n == null) {
1173 |           continue;
1174 |         }
1175 | 
1176 |         if (devMode) {
1177 |           runSanityCheck();
1178 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1179 |             return null;
1180 |           }
1181 |         }
1182 | 
1183 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1184 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1185 | 
1186 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1187 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1188 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1189 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1190 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1191 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1192 |         }
1193 | 
1194 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1195 |       }
1196 | 
1197 |       externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1198 |       externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1199 | 
1200 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1201 |     } finally {
1202 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1203 |     }
1204 |   }

68 |   @Override
69 |   Node optimizeSubtree(Node subtree) {
70 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
71 |       case Token.CALL:
72 |         return tryFoldKnownMethods(subtree);
73 | 
74 |       case Token.NEW:
75 |         return tryFoldCtorCall(subtree);
76 | 
77 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
78 |         return tryFoldTypeof(subtree);
79 |

86 | 
87 |       case Token.VOID:
88 |         return tryReduceVoid(subtree);
89 | 
90 |       default:

92 |         return tryFoldBinaryOperator(subtree);
93 |     }
94 |   }

   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.

/**
   * Converts an operator's token value (see {@link Token}) to a string
   * representation.
   *
   * @param operator the operator's token value to convert
   * @return the string representation or {@code null} if the token value is
   * not an operator

2099 |   static String opToStr(int operator) {
2100 |     switch (operator) {
2101 |       case Token.BITOR: return "|";
2102 |       case Token.OR: return "||";
2103 |       case Token.BITXOR: return "^";
2104 |       case Token.AND: return "&&";

2107 |       case Token.EQ: return "==";
2108 |       case Token.NOT: return "!";
2109 |       case Token.NE: return "!=";
2110 |       case Token.SHNE: return "!==";
2111 |       case Token.LSH: return "<<";

2113 |       case Token.LE: return "<=";
2114 |       case Token.LT: return "<";
2115 |       case Token.URSH: return ">>>";
2116 |       case Token.RSH: return ">>";
2117 |       case Token.GE: return ">=";
2118 |       case Token.GT: return ">";
2119 |       case Token.MUL: return "*";

2121 |       case Token.MOD: return "%";
2122 |       case Token.BITNOT: return "~";
2123 |       case Token.ADD: return "+";
2124 |       case Token.SUB: return "-";
2125 |       case Token.POS: return "+";
2126 |       case Token.NEG: return "-";
2127 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return "=";
2128 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR: return "|=";
2129 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR: return "^=";
2130 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND: return "&=";
2131 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH: return "<<=";
2132 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH: return ">>=";
2133 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH: return ">>>=";
2134 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD: return "+=";
2135 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB: return "-=";
2136 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL: return "*=";
2137 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV: return "/=";
2138 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD: return "%=";
2139 |       case Token.VOID: return "void";
2140 |       case Token.TYPEOF: return "typeof";
2141 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF: return "instanceof";
2142 |       default: return null;
2143 |     }
2144 |   }

===== M0161 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 923-1039
Documentation:

Method code:
923 |   private void toString(
924 |       StringBuilder sb,
925 |       boolean printSource,
926 |       boolean printAnnotations,
927 |       boolean printType) {
928 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
929 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
930 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
931 |         sb.append(' ');
932 |         sb.append(getString());
933 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
934 |         sb.append(' ');
935 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
936 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
937 |         // toStringTree.
938 |         if (first.getType() == Token.STRING) {
939 |           sb.append(first.getString());
940 |         }
941 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
942 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
943 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
944 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
945 |           sb.append(' ');
946 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
947 |         }
948 |         if (printSource) {
949 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
950 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
951 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
952 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
953 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
954 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
955 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
956 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
957 |           sb.append(']');
958 |         }
959 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
960 |         sb.append(' ');
961 |         sb.append(getDouble());
962 |       }
963 |       if (printSource) {
964 |         int lineno = getLineno();
965 |         if (lineno != -1) {
966 |           sb.append(' ');
967 |           sb.append(lineno);
968 |         }
969 |       }
970 | 
971 |       if (printAnnotations) {
972 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
973 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
974 |           int type = keys[i];
975 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
976 |           sb.append(" [");
977 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
978 |           sb.append(": ");
979 |           String value;
980 |           switch (type) {
981 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
982 |               value = "target block property";
983 |               break;
984 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
985 |               value = "last local block";
986 |               break;
987 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
988 |               switch (x.intValue) {
989 |                 case BOTH:
990 |                   value = "both";
991 |                   break;
992 |                 case RIGHT:
993 |                   value = "right";
994 |                   break;
995 |                 case LEFT:
996 |                   value = "left";
997 |                   break;
998 |                 default:
999 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1000 |               }
1001 |               break;
1002 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1003 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1004 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1005 |                   value = "eval";
1006 |                   break;
1007 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1008 |                   value = "with";
1009 |                   break;
1010 |                 default:
1011 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1012 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1013 |               }
1014 |               break;
1015 |             default:
1016 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1017 |               if (obj != null) {
1018 |                 value = obj.toString();
1019 |               } else {
1020 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1021 |               }
1022 |               break;
1023 |           }
1024 |           sb.append(value);
1025 |           sb.append(']');
1026 |         }
1027 |       }
1028 | 
1029 |       if (printType) {
1030 |         if (jsType != null) {
1031 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1032 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1033 |             sb.append(" : ");
1034 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1035 |           }
1036 |         }
1037 |       }
1038 |     }
1039 |   }

===== M0165 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 84-106
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
85 |       String sourceStr) {
86 |     try {
87 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
89 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
90 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
91 |           logger_);
92 |     } catch (IOException e) {
93 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
94 |     }
95 | 
96 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
97 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
98 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
99 |     } else {
100 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
104 |     // the source file and module.
105 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
106 |   }

   * Casts this to an ObjectType, or returns null if this is not an ObjectType.

468 |   private boolean implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown() {
469 |     ObjectType p = getImplicitPrototype();

475 |     }
476 |     return false;
477 |   }

===== M0171 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

154 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
155 |                                    String sourceString,
156 |                                    Config config,
157 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
158 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
159 |         config, errorReporter);

161 | 
162 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {

171 |

173 |   }

===== M0181 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2401-2411
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2401 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2402 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2403 |     int len = s.length();
2404 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2405 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2406 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2407 |         return false;
2408 |       }
2409 |     }
2410 |     return true;
2411 |   }

===== M0199 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldAdd(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 797-817
Documentation:

Method code:
797 |   private Node tryFoldAdd(Node node, Node left, Node right) {
798 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(node.getType() == Token.ADD);
799 | 
800 |     if (NodeUtil.mayBeString(node, true)) {
801 |       if (NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(left, false) &&
802 |           NodeUtil.isLiteralValue(right, false)) {
803 |         // '6' + 7
804 |         return tryFoldAddConstantString(node, left, right);
805 |       } else {
806 |         // a + 7 or 6 + a
807 |         return tryFoldChildAddString(node, left, right);
808 |       }
809 |     } else {
810 |       // Try arithmetic add
811 |       Node result = tryFoldArithmeticOp(node, left, right);
812 |       if (result != node) {
813 |         return result;
814 |       }
815 |       return tryFoldLeftChildOp(node, left, right);
816 |     }
817 |   }

/**
   * @return A detached clone of the Node and all its children.
   */
Method code:
1875 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1876 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1877 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {
1878 |       Node n2clone = n2.cloneTree();
1879 |       n2clone.parent = result;
1880 |       if (result.last != null) {
1881 |         result.last.next = n2clone;
1882 |       }
1883 |       if (result.first == null) {
1884 |         result.first = n2clone;
1885 |       }
1886 |       result.last = n2clone;
1887 |     }
1888 |     return result;
1889 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0203 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1027-1047
Documentation:

Method code:
1027 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1028 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1029 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1030 |       return s;
1031 |     }
1032 | 
1033 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1034 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1035 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1036 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1037 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1038 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1039 |       // crazy.
1040 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1041 |         sb.append(c);
1042 |       } else {
1043 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1044 |       }
1045 |     }
1046 |     return sb.toString();
1047 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1076-1151
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1076 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1077 |     switch (type) {
1078 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1079 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1080 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1081 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1082 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1083 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1084 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1085 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1086 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1087 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1088 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1089 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1090 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1091 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1092 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1093 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1094 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1095 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1096 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1097 |       case Token.EQ:
1098 |       case Token.NE:
1099 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1100 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1101 |       case Token.LT:
1102 |       case Token.GT:
1103 |       case Token.LE:
1104 |       case Token.GE:
1105 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1106 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1107 |       case Token.LSH:
1108 |       case Token.RSH:
1109 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1110 |       case Token.SUB:
1111 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1112 |       case Token.MUL:
1113 |       case Token.MOD:
1114 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1115 |       case Token.INC:
1116 |       case Token.DEC:
1117 |       case Token.NEW:
1118 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1119 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1120 |       case Token.VOID:
1121 |       case Token.NOT:
1122 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1123 |       case Token.POS:
1124 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1125 | 
1126 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1127 |       case Token.CALL:
1128 |       case Token.EMPTY:
1129 |       case Token.FALSE:
1130 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1131 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1132 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1133 |       case Token.GET_REF:

1135 |       case Token.LP:
1136 |       case Token.NAME:
1137 |       case Token.NULL:
1138 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1139 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1140 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1141 |       case Token.RETURN:
1142 |       case Token.STRING:
1143 |       case Token.THIS:
1144 |       case Token.TRUE:
1145 |         return 15;
1146 | 
1147 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1148 |                                Node.tokenToName(type) +
1149 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1150 |     }
1151 |   }

===== M0209 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 734-776
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
734 |   @Override
735 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
736 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
737 |       return true;
738 |     }
739 | 
740 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
741 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
742 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
743 |         return true;
744 |       }
745 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
746 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
747 |         return false;
748 |       }
749 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
750 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
751 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
752 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
753 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
754 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
755 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
756 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
757 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
758 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
759 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
760 | 
761 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
762 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
763 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
764 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
765 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
766 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
767 | 
768 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
769 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
770 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
771 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);

===== M0224 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 221-273
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
221 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
222 |     this.options = options;
223 |     if (errorManager == null) {
224 |       if (outStream == null) {
225 |         setErrorManager(
226 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
227 |       } else {
228 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
229 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
230 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
231 |         setErrorManager(printer);
232 |       }
233 |     }
234 | 
235 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
236 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
237 |     guards.add(
238 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
239 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
240 |     WarningsGuard warningsGuard = options.getWarningsGuard();
241 |     if (warningsGuard != null) {
242 |       guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
243 |     }
244 | 
245 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
246 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
247 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
248 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
249 |     // error off.
250 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
251 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
252 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
253 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
254 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
258 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
259 |       options.checkTypes = true;
260 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
261 |       options.checkTypes = false;
262 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
263 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
264 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
265 |       // parser type warnings.
266 |       guards.add(
267 |           new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
268 |               DiagnosticGroup.forType(
269 |                   RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
270 |               CheckLevel.OFF));
271 |     }
272 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
273 |   }

/**
   * Returns true if the operator is commutative.
   * e.g. (a * b) * c = c * (b * a)
   * Note 1: "+" is not commutative because it is also the concatenation
   * for strings. e.g. "a" + (1 + 2) is not "a" + 1 + 2
   * Note 2: only operations on literals and pure functions are commutative.
   */
Method code:
1343 |   static boolean isCommutative(int type) {
1344 |     switch (type) {
1345 |       case Token.MUL:
1346 |       case Token.BITOR:
1347 |       case Token.BITXOR:
1348 |       case Token.BITAND:
1349 |         return true;
1350 |       default:
1351 |         return false;
1352 |     }
1353 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

===== M0247 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 82-720
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
83 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
84 |       return;
85 |     }
86 | 
87 |     int type = n.getType();
88 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
89 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
90 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
91 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
92 | 
93 |     // Handle all binary operators
94 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
95 |       Preconditions.checkState(
96 |           childCount == 2,
97 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
98 |           opstr, childCount);
99 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
100 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
101 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
102 | 
103 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
104 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
105 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
106 | 
107 |       // Handle associativity.
108 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
109 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
110 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
111 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
112 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
113 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
114 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else {
117 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
118 |       }
119 |       return;
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
123 | 
124 |     switch (type) {
125 |       case Token.TRY: {
126 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
127 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
128 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
129 | 
130 |         add("try");
131 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
132 | 
133 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
134 |         // isn't a catch block
135 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
136 |         if (catchblock != null) {
137 |           add(catchblock);
138 |         }
139 | 
140 |         if (childCount == 3) {
141 |           add("finally");
142 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
143 |         }
144 |         break;
145 |       }
146 | 
147 |       case Token.CATCH:
148 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
149 |         add("catch(");
150 |         add(first);
151 |         add(")");
152 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
153 |         break;
154 | 
155 |       case Token.THROW:
156 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
157 |         add("throw");
158 |         add(first);
159 | 
160 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
161 |         // parse this.
162 |         cc.endStatement(true);
163 |         break;
164 | 
165 |       case Token.RETURN:
166 |         add("return");
167 |         if (childCount == 1) {
168 |           add(first);
169 |         } else {
170 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
171 |         }
172 |         cc.endStatement();
173 |         break;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.VAR:
176 |         if (first != null) {
177 |           add("var ");
178 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
179 |         }
180 |         break;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
183 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
184 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.NAME:
188 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
189 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         } else {
191 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
192 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
193 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
194 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
195 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
196 |           } else {
197 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
198 |             // precedence.
199 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
200 |           }
201 |         }
202 |         break;
203 | 
204 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
205 |         add("[");
206 |         addArrayList(first);
207 |         add("]");
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.LP:
211 |         add("(");
212 |         addList(first);
213 |         add(")");
214 |         break;
215 | 
216 |       case Token.COMMA:
217 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
218 |         addList(first, false, context);
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.NUMBER:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(
223 |             childCount ==
224 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
225 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0));
226 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
227 |         break;
228 | 
229 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
230 |       case Token.VOID:
231 |       case Token.NOT:
232 |       case Token.BITNOT:
233 |       case Token.POS: {
234 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
235 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
236 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
237 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
238 |         break;
239 |       }
240 | 
241 |       case Token.NEG: {
242 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
243 | 
244 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
245 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
246 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
247 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
248 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
249 |         } else {
250 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
251 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         break;
255 |       }
256 | 
257 |       case Token.HOOK: {
258 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
259 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
260 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
261 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
262 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
263 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
264 |         addExpr(last, 1);
265 |         break;
266 |       }
267 | 
268 |       case Token.REGEXP:
269 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||
270 |             last.getType() != Token.STRING) {
271 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
272 |         }
273 | 
274 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
275 | 
276 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
277 |         if (childCount == 2) {
278 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
279 |         } else {
280 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
281 |           add(regexp);
282 |         }
283 |         break;
284 | 
285 |       case Token.GET_REF:
286 |         add(first);
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
290 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
291 |         add(first);
292 |         add(".");
293 |         add((String) n.getProp(Node.NAME_PROP));
294 |         break;
295 | 
296 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
297 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
298 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
299 |         }
300 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
301 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
302 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
303 |           add("(");
304 |         }
305 | 
306 |         add("function");
307 |         add(first);
308 | 
309 |         add(first.getNext());
310 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
311 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
312 | 
313 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
314 |           add(")");
315 |         }
316 |         break;
317 | 
318 |       case Token.GET:
319 |       case Token.SET:
320 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT);
321 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
322 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
323 | 
324 |         // Get methods are unnamed
325 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
326 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
327 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
328 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
329 |           add("get ");
330 |         } else {
331 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
332 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
333 |           add("set ");
334 |         }
335 | 
336 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
337 |         String name = n.getString();
338 |         Node fn = first;
339 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
340 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
341 | 
342 |         // Add the property name.
343 |         if (TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
344 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
345 |             // unicode escaped.
346 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
347 |           add(name);
348 |         } else {
349 |           add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
350 |         }
351 | 
352 |         add(parameters);
353 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
354 |         break;
355 | 
356 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
357 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
358 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
359 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
360 |         }
361 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
362 |         if (preserveBlock) {
363 |           cc.beginBlock();
364 |         }
365 | 
366 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
367 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
368 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
369 |                 !preserveBlock &&
370 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
371 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);
372 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
373 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
374 | 
375 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
376 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
377 |             cc.endStatement();
378 |           }
379 | 
380 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
381 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
382 |           }
383 | 
384 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
385 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
386 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
387 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
388 |           }
389 |         }
390 |         if (preserveBlock) {
391 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
392 |         }
393 |         break;
394 |       }
395 | 
396 |       case Token.FOR:
397 |         if (childCount == 4) {
398 |           add("for(");
399 |           if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
400 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           } else {
402 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
403 |           }
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext());
406 |           add(";");
407 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
408 |           add(")");
409 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
410 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
411 |         } else {
412 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
413 |           add("for(");
414 |           add(first);
415 |           add("in");
416 |           add(first.getNext());
417 |           add(")");
418 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
419 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
420 |         }
421 |         break;
422 | 
423 |       case Token.DO:
424 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
425 |         add("do");
426 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
427 |         add("while(");
428 |         add(last);
429 |         add(")");
430 |         cc.endStatement();
431 |         break;
432 | 
433 |       case Token.WHILE:
434 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
435 |         add("while(");
436 |         add(first);
437 |         add(")");
438 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
439 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
440 |         break;
441 | 
442 |       case Token.EMPTY:
443 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
444 |         break;
445 | 
446 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
447 |         Preconditions.checkState(
448 |             childCount == 2,
449 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
450 |         Preconditions.checkState(
451 |             last.getType() == Token.STRING,
452 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
453 |         boolean needsParens = (first.getType() == Token.NUMBER);
454 |         if (needsParens) {
455 |           add("(");
456 |         }
457 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
458 |         if (needsParens) {
459 |           add(")");
460 |         }
461 |         add(".");
462 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
463 |         break;
464 |       }
465 | 
466 |       case Token.GETELEM:
467 |         Preconditions.checkState(
468 |             childCount == 2,
469 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
470 |         addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
471 |         add("[");
472 |         add(first.getNext());
473 |         add("]");
474 |         break;
475 | 
476 |       case Token.WITH:
477 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
478 |         add("with(");
479 |         add(first);
480 |         add(")");
481 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
482 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
483 |         break;
484 | 
485 |       case Token.INC:
486 |       case Token.DEC: {
487 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
488 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
489 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
490 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
491 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
492 |         if (postProp != 0) {
493 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |         } else {
496 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
497 |           add(first);
498 |         }
499 |         break;
500 |       }
501 | 
502 |       case Token.CALL:
503 |         // We have two special cases here:
504 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
505 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
506 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
507 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
508 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
509 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
510 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
511 |         // that must be preserved.
512 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
513 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
514 |           add("(0,");
515 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
516 |           add(")");
517 |         } else {
518 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
519 |         }
520 |         add("(");
521 |         addList(first.getNext());
522 |         add(")");
523 |         break;
524 | 
525 |       case Token.IF:
526 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
527 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
528 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
529 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
530 |           cc.beginBlock();
531 |         }
532 | 
533 |         add("if(");
534 |         add(first);
535 |         add(")");
536 | 
537 |         if (hasElse) {
538 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
539 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
540 |           add("else");
541 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
542 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
543 |         } else {
544 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
545 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
546 |         }
547 | 
548 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
549 |           cc.endBlock();
550 |         }
551 |         break;
552 | 
553 |       case Token.NULL:
554 |       case Token.THIS:
555 |       case Token.FALSE:
556 |       case Token.TRUE:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
563 |         add("continue");
564 |         if (childCount == 1) {
565 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
566 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
567 |           }
568 |           add(" ");
569 |           add(first);
570 |         }
571 |         cc.endStatement();
572 |         break;
573 | 
574 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
575 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
576 |         add("debugger");
577 |         cc.endStatement();
578 |         break;
579 | 
580 |       case Token.BREAK:
581 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
582 |         add("break");
583 |         if (childCount == 1) {
584 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
585 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
586 |           }
587 |           add(" ");
588 |           add(first);
589 |         }
590 |         cc.endStatement();
591 |         break;
592 | 
593 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
594 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
595 | 
596 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
597 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
598 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
599 |         cc.endStatement();
600 |         break;
601 | 
602 |       case Token.NEW:
603 |         add("new ");
604 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
605 | 
606 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
607 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
608 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
609 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
610 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
611 |         }
612 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
613 | 
614 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
615 |         Node next = first.getNext();
616 |         if (next != null) {
617 |           add("(");
618 |           addList(next);
619 |           add(")");
620 |         }
621 |         break;
622 | 
623 |       case Token.STRING:
624 |         if (childCount !=
625 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
626 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
627 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
628 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
629 |         }
630 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
631 |         break;
632 | 
633 |       case Token.DELPROP:
634 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
635 |         add("delete ");
636 |         add(first);
637 |         break;
638 | 
639 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
640 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
641 |         if (needsParens) {
642 |           add("(");
643 |         }
644 |         add("{");
645 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
646 |           if (c != first) {
647 |             cc.listSeparator();
648 |           }
649 | 
650 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
651 |             add(c);
652 |           } else {
653 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they are
654 |             // not JavaScript keywords
655 |             if (c.getType() == Token.STRING &&
656 |                 !c.isQuotedString() &&
657 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(c.getString()) &&
658 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(c.getString()) &&
659 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
660 |                 // unicode escaped.
661 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(c.getString())) {
662 |               add(c.getString());
663 |             } else {
664 |               addExpr(c, 1);
665 |             }
666 |             add(":");
667 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
668 |           }
669 |         }
670 |         add("}");
671 |         if (needsParens) {
672 |           add(")");
673 |         }
674 |         break;
675 |       }
676 | 
677 |       case Token.SWITCH:
678 |         add("switch(");
679 |         add(first);
680 |         add(")");
681 |         cc.beginBlock();
682 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
683 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
684 |         break;
685 | 
686 |       case Token.CASE:
687 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
688 |         add("case ");
689 |         add(first);
690 |         addCaseBody(last);
691 |         break;
692 | 
693 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
694 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
695 |         add("default");
696 |         addCaseBody(first);
697 |         break;
698 | 
699 |       case Token.LABEL:
700 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
701 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
702 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
703 |         }
704 |         add(first);
705 |         add(":");
706 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
707 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
711 |       // ignored for our purposes.
712 |       case Token.SETNAME:
713 |         break;
714 | 
715 |       default:
716 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
717 |     }
718 | 
719 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
720 |   }
