===== M0256 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 96-163
Documentation:

Method code:
96 |   private Node tryFoldBinaryOperator(Node subtree) {
97 |     Node left = subtree.getFirstChild();
98 | 
99 |     if (left == null) {
100 |       return subtree;
101 |     }
102 | 
103 |     Node right = left.getNext();
104 | 
105 |     if (right == null) {
106 |       return subtree;
107 |     }
108 | 
109 |     // If we've reached here, node is truly a binary operator.
110 |     switch(subtree.getType()) {
111 |       case Token.GETPROP:
112 |         return tryFoldGetProp(subtree, left, right);
113 | 
114 |       case Token.GETELEM:
115 |         return tryFoldGetElem(subtree, left, right);
116 | 
117 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
118 |         return tryFoldInstanceof(subtree, left, right);
119 | 
120 |       case Token.AND:
121 |       case Token.OR:
122 |         return tryFoldAndOr(subtree, left, right);
123 | 
124 |       case Token.LSH:
125 |       case Token.RSH:
126 |       case Token.URSH:
127 |         return tryFoldShift(subtree, left, right);
128 | 
129 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
130 |         return tryFoldAssign(subtree, left, right);
131 | 
132 |       case Token.ADD:
133 |         return tryFoldAdd(subtree, left, right);
134 | 
135 |       case Token.SUB:
136 |       case Token.DIV:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |         return tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
139 | 
140 |       case Token.MUL:
141 |       case Token.BITAND:
142 |       case Token.BITOR:
143 |       case Token.BITXOR:
144 |         Node result = tryFoldArithmeticOp(subtree, left, right);
145 |         if (result != subtree) {
146 |           return result;
147 |         }
148 |         return tryFoldLeftChildOp(subtree, left, right);
149 | 
150 |       case Token.LT:
151 |       case Token.GT:
152 |       case Token.LE:
153 |       case Token.GE:
154 |       case Token.EQ:
155 |       case Token.NE:
156 |       case Token.SHEQ:
157 |       case Token.SHNE:
158 |         return tryFoldComparison(subtree, left, right);
159 | 
160 |       default:
161 |         return subtree;
162 |     }
163 |   }

===== M0257 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeCutLine()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 459-475
Documentation:
/**
     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
459 |     @Override
460 |     void maybeCutLine() {
461 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
462 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
463 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
464 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
465 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
466 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
467 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition);
468 |           lineIndex++;
469 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
470 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
471 |         } else {
472 |           startNewLine();
473 |         }
474 |       }
475 |     }

===== M0262 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1396-1415
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1396 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1397 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1398 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1399 |     }
1400 | 
1401 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1402 |     Node n, n2;
1403 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1404 |          res == null && n != null;
1405 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1406 |       if (node2 == null) {
1407 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1408 |       }
1409 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1410 |       if (res != null) {
1411 |         return res;
1412 |       }
1413 |     }
1414 |     return res;
1415 |   }

===== M0292 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1146-1154
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1146 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1147 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1148 |       return -1;
1149 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1150 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1151 |     } else {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1153 |     }
1154 |   }

===== M0308 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 163-184
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {
173 |       int size = alternates.size();
174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }

===== M0317 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeOptimizationsPass.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeOptimizationsPass.java
Original range: 61-83
Documentation:

Method code:
61 |   @Override
62 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
63 |     Node currentVersionOfNode = n;
64 |     boolean somethingChanged = false;
65 | 
66 |     do {
67 |       somethingChanged = false;
68 |       for (AbstractPeepholeOptimization optimization : peepholeOptimizations) {
69 |         Node newVersionOfNode =
70 |             optimization.optimizeSubtree(currentVersionOfNode);
71 | 
72 |         if (newVersionOfNode != currentVersionOfNode) {
73 |           somethingChanged = true;
74 | 
75 |           currentVersionOfNode = newVersionOfNode;
76 |         }
77 | 
78 |         if (currentVersionOfNode == null) {
79 |           return;
80 |         }
81 |       }
82 |     } while(somethingChanged);
83 |   }

153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:

155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {
206 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }
211 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
212 |       case Token.FALSE:
213 |       case Token.NULL:
214 |       case Token.THIS:
215 |       case Token.TRUE:
216 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
223 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
229 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:
235 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
236 |       case Token.IF:
237 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
238 |       case Token.LABEL:
239 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
240 |       case Token.LP:
241 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
242 |       case Token.NAME:

244 |       case Token.NEW:
245 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
246 |       case Token.NUMBER:

248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:

254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:

256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.CONST:
265 |       case Token.VAR:
266 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
267 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
268 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
269 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
270 |         } else {

272 |                                           node.getClass() +
273 |                                           " type: " +
274 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
275 |         }
276 |       case Token.WHILE:
277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:
279 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
280 |     }
281 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
282 |   }

===== M0337 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0351 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.PeepholeFoldConstants.tryFoldLeftChildOp(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/PeepholeFoldConstants.java
Original range: 757-795
Documentation:
/**
   * Expressions such as [foo() * 10 * 20] generate parse trees
   * where no node has two const children ((foo() * 10) * 20), so
   * performArithmeticOp() won't fold it -- tryFoldLeftChildOp() will.
   * Specifically it folds associative expressions where:
   *  - The left child is also an associative expression of the same time.
   *  - The right child is a constant NUMBER constant.
   *  - The left child's right child is a NUMBER constant.
   */
Method code:
757 |   private Node tryFoldLeftChildOp(Node n, Node left, Node right) {
758 |     int opType = n.getType();
759 |     Preconditions.checkState(
760 |         (NodeUtil.isAssociative(opType) && NodeUtil.isCommutative(opType))
761 |         || n.getType() == Token.ADD);
762 | 
763 |     Preconditions.checkState(
764 |         n.getType() != Token.ADD || !NodeUtil.mayBeString(n));
765 | 
766 |     // Use getNumberValue to handle constants like "NaN" and "Infinity"
767 |     // other values are converted to numbers elsewhere.
768 |     Double rightValObj = NodeUtil.getNumberValue(right);
769 |     if (rightValObj != null && left.getType() == opType) {
770 |       Preconditions.checkState(left.getChildCount() == 2);
771 | 
772 |       Node ll = left.getFirstChild();
773 |       Node lr = ll.getNext();
774 | 
775 |       Node valueToCombine = ll;
776 |       Node replacement = performArithmeticOp(opType, valueToCombine, right);
777 |       if (replacement == null) {
778 |         valueToCombine = lr;
779 |         replacement = performArithmeticOp(opType, valueToCombine, right);
780 |       }
781 |       if (replacement != null) {
782 |         // Remove the child that has been combined
783 |         left.removeChild(valueToCombine);
784 |         // Replace the left op with the remaining child.
785 |         n.replaceChild(left, left.removeFirstChild());
786 |         // New "-Infinity" node need location info explicitly
787 |         // added.
788 |         replacement.copyInformationFromForTree(right);
789 |         n.replaceChild(right, replacement);
790 |         reportCodeChange();
791 |       }
792 |     }
793 | 
794 |     return n;
795 |   }

===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.valueCheck(Node,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1157-1172
Documentation:
/**
   * Apply the supplied predicate against the potential
   * all possible result of the expression.
   */
Method code:
1157 |   static boolean valueCheck(Node n, Predicate<Node> p) {
1158 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1159 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
1160 |       case Token.COMMA:
1161 |         return valueCheck(n.getLastChild(), p);
1162 |       case Token.AND:
1163 |       case Token.OR:
1164 |         return valueCheck(n.getFirstChild(), p)
1165 |             && valueCheck(n.getLastChild(), p);
1166 |       case Token.HOOK:
1167 |         return valueCheck(n.getFirstChild().getNext(), p)
1168 |             && valueCheck(n.getLastChild(), p);
1169 |       default:
1170 |         return p.apply(n);
1171 |     }
1172 |   }

===== M0365 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

===== M0371 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 400-415
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
400 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
401 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
402 |       Set<String> directives = null;
403 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
404 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
405 |         if (directives == null) {
406 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
407 |         } else {
408 |           directives.add(directive);
409 |         }
410 |       }
411 | 
412 |       if (directives != null) {
413 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
414 |       }
415 |     }

439 |   void initInputsByNameMap() {

===== M0377 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 422-466
Documentation:

Method code:
422 |   @Override
423 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
424 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
425 |       return true;
426 |     }
427 | 
428 |     // Union types
429 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
430 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
431 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
432 |       return false;
433 |     }
434 | 
435 |     // record types
436 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
437 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Interfaces
441 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
442 |     // to the interface instance.
443 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
444 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
445 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
446 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
447 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
448 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // other prototype based objects
455 |     if (that != null) {
456 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
457 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
458 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
459 |         // to avoid guessing.
460 |         return true;
461 |       }
462 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
463 |     }
464 | 
465 |     return false;
466 |   }

===== M0383 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

393 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(right.parent == null,

395 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(right.next == null,
396 |         "second new child has existing sibling");
397 |     type = nodeType;
398 |     parent = null;
399 |     first = left;
400 |     last = right;
401 |     left.next = right;
402 |     left.parent = this;
403 |     right.next = null;
404 |     right.parent = this;
405 |     sourcePosition = -1;
406 |   }

===== M0394 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 286-310
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
286 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
287 |     if (prototype == null) {
288 |       return false;
289 |     }
290 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
291 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
292 |       return false;
293 |     }
294 | 
295 |     boolean replacedPrototype = prototype != null;
296 |     this.prototype = prototype;
297 | 
298 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
299 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
300 |       if (superClass != null) {
301 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
302 |       }
303 |     }
304 | 
305 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
306 |       clearCachedValues();
307 |     }
308 | 
309 |     return true;
310 |   }

Original range: 176-214
Documentation:

Method code:
176 |   private void tryReduceOperandsForOp(Node n) {
177 |     switch (n.getType()) {
178 |       case Token.ADD:
179 |         Node left = n.getFirstChild();
180 |         Node right = n.getLastChild();
181 |         if (!NodeUtil.mayBeString(left) && !NodeUtil.mayBeString(right)) {
182 |           tryConvertOperandsToNumber(n);
183 |         }
184 |         break;
185 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
186 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
187 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
188 |         // TODO(johnlenz): convert these to integers.
189 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
190 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
191 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
192 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
193 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
194 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
195 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
196 |         tryConvertToNumber(n.getLastChild());
197 |         break;
198 |       case Token.BITNOT:
199 |       case Token.BITOR:
200 |       case Token.BITXOR:
201 |       case Token.BITAND:
202 |       case Token.LSH:
203 |       case Token.RSH:
204 |       case Token.URSH:
205 |       case Token.SUB:
206 |       case Token.MUL:
207 |       case Token.MOD:
208 |       case Token.DIV:
209 |       case Token.POS:
210 |       case Token.NEG:
211 |         tryConvertOperandsToNumber(n);
212 |         break;
213 |     }
214 |   }

/**
   * Defines a property whose type is synthesized (i.e. not inferred).
   * @param propertyName the property's name
   * @param type the type
   * @param inExterns {@code true} if this property was defined in an externs
   *        file. TightenTypes assumes that any function passed to an externs
   *        property could be called, so setting this incorrectly could result
   *        in live code being removed.
   * @param propertyNode the node corresponding to the declaration of property
   *        which might later be accessed using {@code getPropertyNode}.
   */
Method code:
247 |   public final boolean defineDeclaredProperty(String propertyName,
248 |       JSType type, boolean inExterns, Node propertyNode) {
249 |     boolean result = defineProperty(propertyName, type, false, inExterns,
250 |         propertyNode);
251 | 
252 |     // All property definitions go through this method
253 |     // or defineDeclaredProperty. Because the properties defined an an
254 |     // object can affect subtyping, it's slightly more efficient
255 |     // to register this after defining the property.
256 |     registry.registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, this);
257 | 
258 |     return result;
259 |   }

===== M0448 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 411-427
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
411 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
412 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
413 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
414 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
415 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
416 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
417 |         String inputName = input.getName();
418 | 
419 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
420 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
421 |         // will get an error down the line.
422 |         inputs.add(input);
423 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 |     return inputs;
427 |   }

/**
   * Adds a node at the left-hand side of an expression. Unlike
   * {@link #addExpr(Node,int)}, this preserves information about the context.
   *
   * The left side of an expression is special because in the JavaScript
   * grammar, certain tokens may be parsed differently when they are at
   * the beginning of a statement. For example, "{}" is parsed as a block,
   * but "{'x': 'y'}" is parsed as an object literal.
   */
Method code:
830 |   void addLeftExpr(Node n, int minPrecedence, Context context) {
831 |     addExpr(n, minPrecedence, context);
832 |   }

===== M0462 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 467-481
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
467 |   @Override
468 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
469 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
470 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
471 |     if (unknown) {
472 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
473 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
474 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
475 |         unknown = false;
476 |       } else {
477 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
478 |       }
479 |     }
480 |     return unknown;
481 |   }

===== M0463 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.checkForStateChangeHelper(Node,boolean,AbstractCompiler)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 699-842
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if some node in n's subtree changes application state.
   * If {@code checkForNewObjects} is true, we assume that newly created
   * mutable objects (like object literals) change state. Otherwise, we assume
   * that they have no side effects.
   */
Method code:
699 |   private static boolean checkForStateChangeHelper(
700 |       Node n, boolean checkForNewObjects, AbstractCompiler compiler) {
701 |     // Rather than id which ops may have side effects, id the ones
702 |     // that we know to be safe
703 |     switch (n.getType()) {
704 |       // other side-effect free statements and expressions
705 |       case Token.AND:
706 |       case Token.BLOCK:
707 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
708 |       case Token.HOOK:
709 |       case Token.IF:
710 |       case Token.IN:
711 |       case Token.LP:
712 |       case Token.NUMBER:
713 |       case Token.OR:
714 |       case Token.THIS:
715 |       case Token.TRUE:
716 |       case Token.FALSE:
717 |       case Token.NULL:
718 |       case Token.STRING:
719 |       case Token.SWITCH:
720 |       case Token.TRY:
721 |       case Token.EMPTY:
722 |         break;
723 | 
724 |       // Throws are by definition side effects
725 |       case Token.THROW:
726 |         return true;
727 | 
728 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
729 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
730 |           return true;
731 |         }
732 |         for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
733 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
734 |                   c.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
735 |             return true;
736 |           }
737 |         }
738 |         return false;
739 | 
740 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
741 |       case Token.REGEXP:
742 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
743 |           return true;
744 |         }
745 |         break;
746 | 
747 |       case Token.VAR:    // empty var statement (no declaration)
748 |       case Token.NAME:   // variable by itself
749 |         if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
750 |           return true;
751 |         }
752 |         break;
753 | 
754 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
755 |         // Function expressions don't have side-effects, but function
756 |         // declarations change the namespace. Either way, we don't need to
757 |         // check the children, since they aren't executed at declaration time.
758 |         return checkForNewObjects || !isFunctionExpression(n);
759 | 
760 |       case Token.NEW:
761 |         if (checkForNewObjects) {
762 |           return true;
763 |         }
764 | 
765 |         if (!constructorCallHasSideEffects(n)) {
766 |           // loop below will see if the constructor parameters have
767 |           // side-effects
768 |           break;
769 |         }
770 |         return true;
771 | 
772 |       case Token.CALL:
773 |         // calls to functions that have no side effects have the no
774 |         // side effect property set.
775 |         if (!functionCallHasSideEffects(n, compiler)) {
776 |           // loop below will see if the function parameters have
777 |           // side-effects
778 |           break;
779 |         }
780 |         return true;
781 | 
782 |       default:
783 |         if (isSimpleOperatorType(n.getType())) {
784 |           break;
785 |         }
786 | 
787 |         if (isAssignmentOp(n)) {
788 |           Node assignTarget = n.getFirstChild();
789 |           if (isName(assignTarget)) {
790 |             return true;
791 |           }
792 | 
793 |           // Assignments will have side effects if
794 |           // a) The RHS has side effects, or
795 |           // b) The LHS has side effects, or
796 |           // c) A name on the LHS will exist beyond the life of this statement.
797 |           if (checkForStateChangeHelper(
798 |                   n.getFirstChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler) ||
799 |               checkForStateChangeHelper(
800 |                   n.getLastChild(), checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
801 |             return true;
802 |           }
803 | 
804 |           if (isGet(assignTarget)) {
805 |             // If the object being assigned to is a local object, don't
806 |             // consider this a side-effect as it can't be referenced
807 |             // elsewhere.  Don't do this recursively as the property might
808 |             // be an alias of another object, unlike a literal below.
809 |             Node current = assignTarget.getFirstChild();
810 |             if (evaluatesToLocalValue(current)) {
811 |               return false;
812 |             }
813 | 
814 |             // A literal value as defined by "isLiteralValue" is guaranteed
815 |             // not to be an alias, or any components which are aliases of
816 |             // other objects.
817 |             // If the root object is a literal don't consider this a
818 |             // side-effect.
819 |             while (isGet(current)) {
820 |               current = current.getFirstChild();
821 |             }
822 | 
823 |             return !isLiteralValue(current, true);
824 |           } else {
825 |             // TODO(johnlenz): remove this code and make this an exception. This
826 |             // is here only for legacy reasons, the AST is not valid but
827 |             // preserve existing behavior.
828 |             return !isLiteralValue(assignTarget, true);
829 |           }
830 |         }
831 | 
832 |         return true;
833 |     }
834 | 
835 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
836 |       if (checkForStateChangeHelper(c, checkForNewObjects, compiler)) {
837 |         return true;
838 |       }
839 |     }
840 | 
841 |     return false;
842 |   }

===== M0465 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getNumberValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 242-299
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a Number, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null Double, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>Number()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
242 |   static Double getNumberValue(Node n) {
243 |     switch (n.getType()) {
244 |       case Token.TRUE:
245 |         return 1.0;
246 | 
247 |       case Token.FALSE:
248 |       case Token.NULL:
249 |         return 0.0;
250 | 
251 |       case Token.NUMBER:
252 |         return n.getDouble();
253 | 
254 |       case Token.VOID:
255 |         if (mayHaveSideEffects(n.getFirstChild())) {
256 |           return null;
257 |         } else {
258 |           return Double.NaN;
259 |         }
260 | 
261 |       case Token.NAME:
262 |         // Check for known constants
263 |         String name = n.getString();
264 |         if (name.equals("undefined")) {
265 |           return Double.NaN;
266 |         }
267 |         if (name.equals("NaN")) {
268 |           return Double.NaN;
269 |         }
270 |         if (name.equals("Infinity")) {
271 |           return Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
272 |         }
273 |         return null;
274 | 
275 |       case Token.NEG:
276 |         if (n.getChildCount() == 1 && n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME
277 |             && n.getFirstChild().getString().equals("Infinity")) {
278 |           return Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
279 |         }
280 |         return null;
281 | 
282 |       case Token.NOT:
283 |         TernaryValue child = getBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
284 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
285 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? 0.0 : 1.0; // reversed.
286 |         }
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.STRING:
290 |         return getStringNumberValue(n.getString());
291 | 
292 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
293 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
294 |         String value = getStringValue(n);
295 |         return value != null ? getStringNumberValue(value) : null;
296 |     }
297 | 
298 |     return null;
299 |   }

===== M0477 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.replaceChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 694-715
Documentation:
/**
   * Detaches child from Node and replaces it with newChild.
   */
Method code:
694 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {
695 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.next == null,
696 |         "The new child node has siblings.");
697 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(newChild.parent == null,
698 |         "The new child node already has a parent.");
699 | 
700 |     // Copy over important information.
701 |     newChild.copyInformationFrom(child);
702 | 
703 |     newChild.next = child.next;
704 |     newChild.parent = this;
705 |     if (child == first) {
706 |         first = newChild;
707 |     } else {
708 |         Node prev = getChildBefore(child);
709 |         prev.next = newChild;
710 |     }
711 |     if (child == last)
712 |         last = newChild;
713 |     child.next = null;
714 |     child.parent = null;
715 |   }
